## 待接入的律 / join candidate (59)

按主题分组（AI 的分组，见 `development/integration-i4a/themes.json`）。Ren 2026-10-16 选择先接入最近的缺口，一般数学与推导演算暂留脊柱之外（[来源](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-i4-decisions.md)）。 / Grouped by theme (an AI grouping, in `development/integration-i4a/themes.json`). On 16 October 2026 Ren chose to join the closest gaps first and to leave general mathematics and the derivation calculus outside the spine for now ([source](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-i4-decisions.md)).

### 更新与闭合 / Renewal and closure (8)

| 模块 / Module | 脊柱 / Spine | 关系 / Relation | 理由 / Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| [RenewalAccounting](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalAccounting.lean) | `Becoming.Generation`, `Becoming.Interdependence` | — | 对每个有限类约束–流之律：各类寿命内打开少于形成所需时，更新势每拍下降，没有历史永远更新。是 RenewalCharacterization 期限的情形；脊柱无此计量。 / For every finite-kind constraint–flow law whose kinds open less over their spans than forming them demands, a renewal potential falls each tick, so no history renews forever. A case of the deadline in RenewalCharacterization; the spine states no such accounting. |
| [RenewalCharacterization](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalCharacterization.lean) | `Becoming.Generation`, `Becoming.Interdependence` | — | 更新–闭合定理：对有限类约束–流之律，有历史永远存续，恰当有非空种群在持久支持下闭合且所开不少于形成所需，否则有期限。 / Renewal–closure theorem: for every finite-kind constraint–flow law, some history stays live forever iff a nonempty population is closed under lasting support and opens at least its formation demand; otherwise an explicit deadline. The spine states only necessity-side laws. |
| [RenewalClosure](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalClosure.lean) | `Becoming.Descent`, `Becoming.Wholes` | — | 对每个有限类、支持范围有界的系统：足够晚的发生源自已实现的支持循环（闭合），无环支持有期限。脊柱的 Embodied.descends 只给一步世系。 / For every occurrence-support system with finitely many kinds and bounded support reach, any late occurrence descends from a realized support cycle (a closure), so acyclic support has a deadline. The spine's Embodied.descends gives one-step descent only. |
| [RenewalConfigurations](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalConfigurations.lean) | `Becoming.Viability.kernel_step_iff` | — | 对每个通道律：配置可更新恰当它存续且一步到可更新者；可更新性从不生成。Viability 只有全称的生存核（kernel_step_iff），无此存在形式。 / For every channel law, a configuration is renewable iff live with a step to a renewable one, and renewability is never generated along a history. Viability states only the universal kernel form (`kernel_step_iff`), not this existential one. |
| [RenewalLink](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalLink.lean) | `Becoming.Descent` | — | 在同一执行上连接 RenewalAccounting 与 RenewalClosure：每个记号在确定的较早一拍形成，故足够晚的存续配置源自已实现的闭合。是更新–闭合定理的组成部分。 / Joins RenewalAccounting and RenewalClosure on one execution: every token was formed on a definite earlier tick, so a live configuration late enough descends from a realized closure. A component of the renewal–closure theorem. |
| [RenewalSufficiency](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalSufficiency.lean) | `Becoming.Generation` | — | 充分性：对每个通道律，持久支持下闭合且打开覆盖形成的非空种群有每拍返回的稳态配置，故永远更新。脊柱的 Generation 在必要性一侧。 / Sufficiency direction: for every channel law, a nonempty population closed under lasting support whose openings cover its formations has a steady configuration that returns each tick, so it renews forever. The spine's Generation laws are necessity-side. |
| [ResourceContinuation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/ResourceContinuation.lean) | `Becoming.Stock` | — | 对每个过程：每次转移后所持加所化不超过之前所持加所来（容许损失），界定轨迹、到达窗口与频率。Stock 只有等式平衡与单纯下降。 / For every process, a per-transition law held-after plus enacted at most held-before plus arrived (losses allowed) bounds every trace, arrival windows and frequencies. Stock states equality balance (`trace_balance`), pure falling (`trace_falls`) and `replenishes_of_draws`, not this inequality form. |
| [SupportNecessity](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/SupportNecessity.lean) | `Becoming.Descent` | — | 对出生需先前仍运作之亲代的每个情节系统：有限寿命与支持类型上有界严格秩给出期限。RenewalClosure 去掉了秩；脊柱的 Embodied.descends 没有期限。 / For every episode system whose births need an earlier functioning parent, finite lifetimes and a bounded strict rank on support kinds give a deadline. RenewalClosure removes the rank; the spine's Embodied.descends states descent but no deadline. |

### 比较、组合与描述 / Comparison, composition and description (10)

| 模块 / Module | 脊柱 / Spine | 关系 / Relation | 理由 / Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| [CoupledOccurrence](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/CoupledOccurrence.lean) | `Becoming.Unfolding` | — | 对每个过程与报告：以同一发生读出的报告标注每次转移，轨迹恰是原律的轨迹（`described_trace_exact`）。Unfolding 的 `withMove` 把动作读入配置，不陈述此律。 / For every process and report, labelling each transition with a report read from that same occurrence keeps exactly the law's traces (`described_trace_exact`, append, view maps). Unfolding's `withMove` reads the move inside the configuration but states no such labelling law. |
| [ExplanatoryComposition](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/ExplanatoryComposition.lean) | `Becoming.Joining.common_operation`, `Becoming.Readout.futures_through_iff`, `Becoming.Bridge.exact_iff_fiber_future_by_core` | — | 最近：Becoming.Joining 与 Readout。可加入：所给目标语言的双侧语境同余，在精确约化表示上带出唯一复合。 / Nearest: Becoming.Joining and Readout. Would add the two-sided context congruence of a supplied target language, carrying one composition on exact reduced presentations. |
| [ExplanatoryHistoryInterfaces](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/ExplanatoryHistoryInterfaces.lean) | `Becoming.Joining.lifts_iff`, `Becoming.JoiningBridge.contextSame_iff_sameTests` | — | 最近：Becoming.Joining。可加入：对任一过程，串接历史在全部接合语境同答当且仅当起止相同，精确叙述经端点分解。wisdom-root 记录引用之。 / Nearest: Becoming.Joining. Would add, for every process, that concatenated histories agree in all joining contexts exactly when they share start and finish, and exact accounts factor through endpoints. Cited by wisdom-root. |
| [ExplanatoryRecovery](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/ExplanatoryRecovery.lean) | `Becoming.Readout.reading_single_iff`, `Becoming.Readout.decodes_iff_of_section` | — | 最近：Becoming.Readout。可加入：过去谓词的关系性恢复：恰在相容前驱一致时可恢复，恢复可复合，合并保留歧义。 / Nearest: Becoming.Readout. Would add relational recovery of a past predicate: recoverable exactly when consistent predecessors agree, recovery composes, and a merge keeps ambiguity. |
| [FormationSymmetry](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/FormationSymmetry.lean) | `Becoming.Joining.openMap_iff` | — | 最近：Joining 的开映射与 Becoming.Branching。可加入：保持步的自映射若固定起点，则固定每个唯一确定的终点，故新形成的不对称需分支或不对称种子。 / Nearest: Joining's open maps and Becoming.Branching. Would add that a step-preserving self-map fixing the start fixes each uniquely determined end, so newly formed asymmetry needs branching or an asymmetric seed. |
| [GeneratedContraction](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/GeneratedContraction.lean) | `Becoming.Frame.Reach.trans`, `Becoming.Frame.Reach.refl` | — | 最近：Frame.Reach。可加入：对带语境运算、框架相容与左单位的过程，替换恰与可达对应，收缩恰与可达复制对应。 / Nearest: Frame.Reach. Would add, for a process with a context operation, frame compatibility and a left unit, that replacement holds exactly with reach and contraction with reachable duplication. |
| [RelationalContinuation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/RelationalContinuation.lean) | `Becoming.Histories.live`, `Becoming.Histories.admits_live_iff` | — | 最近：Becoming.Histories（活步）。可加入：续行即每员都能再步入的区域；沿步保持，由无尽运行推出，互模拟下不变，核心抹去标签即单一循环。 / Nearest: Becoming.Histories (live steps). Would add continuation as a region each member of which steps back into it: kept along steps, implied by unending runs, invariant under bisimulation, its core erased to one recurrence. |
| [RelationalFormation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/RelationalFormation.lean) | `Becoming.Description.Rectangular.image_of_separate`, `Becoming.Description.copy_relates`, `Becoming.Description.Rectangular.of_image_injective` | — | 最近：Becoming.Description（以函数移动各部分）。可加入：分离的关系性多步（非确定）保持矩形，故新限制排除可分解；选择无耦合也能限制。 / Nearest: Becoming.Description, whose laws move each part by a function. Would add separate relational (nondeterministic, type-changing) steps over any number of steps keeping rectangularity, so a new restriction excludes factorization; selection restricts without coupling. |
| [SystemIndividuation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/SystemIndividuation.lean) | `Becoming.Description.separation_faithful_iff`, `Becoming.Description.Rectangular` | — | 最近：Becoming.Description。可加入：坐标集对响应的充分性单调，在域可重组处对交封闭；在相关的域上有两个不可比的极小支撑而无最小者。 / Nearest: Becoming.Description. Would add sufficiency of coordinate sets for a response: monotone, closed under intersection where the domain recombines them, and on a correlated domain two incomparable minimal supports with no least one. |
| [EmbeddedEvaluation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/EmbeddedEvaluation.lean) | `Becoming.Unfolding` | — | 初等的一般规律：每步按一关系更新的记录恰等于该关系沿整段历史成立（`record_tracks_whole_history`），且不改变可行性。脊柱未陈述此律。 / An elementary general law: a record updated by a relation across each step equals that relation along the whole history (`record_tracks_whole_history`) and changes no enabledness or feasibility. The spine's withMove and Entropy records state no such law. |

### 探究与判断 / Inquiry and judgment (9)

| 模块 / Module | 脊柱 / Spine | 关系 / Relation | 理由 / Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| [AttainableQuestions](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/inquiry-root/sufficiency/AttainableQuestions.lean) | `Becoming.Inquiry.commonResponse_judge_iff`, `Becoming.Inquiry.Scope.secures` | — | 对每个过程陈述可靠探究可得问题之序：向下封闭，并恰由共同策略可得，缺并时无最大问题，叶处续接。Becoming.Inquiry 读 InquirySufficiency 但无此。 / States, for every process, the order of questions a sound inquiry attains: downward closure, a join attained exactly by a shared policy, no greatest question when a join is missing, continuation at leaves. Becoming.Inquiry reads InquirySufficiency without this. |
| [InquiryAdequacy](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/inquiry-root/InquiryAdequacy.lean) | `Becoming.Inquiry.commonResponse_judge_iff`, `Becoming.Readout.decodes_iff_of_section` | — | 对任意历史、证据与可接受关系：按证据的适当策略存在，恰当每个证据类有共同可接受选择。脊柱只陈述单一类与带截面的函数情形。 / States, for any histories, evidence and admissibility relation, that an evidence-indexed adequate policy exists exactly when each evidence class has a common admissible choice. The spine states one class and the functional case with a section. |
| [ReachedInquiryComposition](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/inquiry-root/sufficiency/ReachedInquiryComposition.lean) | `Becoming.Inquiry.commonResponse_judge_iff`, `Becoming.Inquiry.Scope.secures` | — | 对每个过程精确刻画在可靠探究已达叶处续接何时识别问题，不需全局保持一致。可与 AttainableQuestions 一同接入 Becoming.Inquiry 附近。 / Characterizes exactly, for every process, when continuing a sound inquiry at its reached leaves identifies a question, without global agreement preservation. Would join with AttainableQuestions near Becoming.Inquiry. |
| [PremiseNecessity](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/PremiseNecessity.lean) | `Becoming.Metaethics` | — | 对每个过程，接续序与其逆都具有前序、随附与组合判据，故需要方向输入。Metaethics 为种子与中立给出必要性见证，没有方向这一项。 / For every process, the continuation order and its converse both carry the preorder, supervenience and composition credentials, so an orientation input is needed. Metaethics states necessity witnesses for its own premises (seed, neutralities), not for orientation. |
| [CorrectionRelations](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/generative-capacity/CorrectionRelations.lean) | `Becoming.Contextual.not_glues`, `Becoming.Contextual.glues_iff_agree` | — | 任意关系的关系边界代数（耦合、精确边界、细化）与奇偶律：相等与相异关系的环有联合赋值当且仅当为偶。Context 以 NoGlobal 为前提；此判据与代数未陈述。Mathlib。 / Algebra of relational boundaries for arbitrary relations (coupling, exact boundaries, refinement) and a parity law: a cycle of equal and differ relations has a joint assignment iff even. Becoming.Context assumes NoGlobal; this criterion and algebra are unstated. Mathlib. |
| [SelectorReviewCoverage](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/generative-capacity/SelectorReviewCoverage.lean) | `Becoming.robust_iff_commonResponse`, `Becoming.commonResponse_antitone` | — | 任意世界、菜单与比较：一描述在所有菜单上覆盖另一描述的清晰证书，当且仅当否定逐对反映；覆盖可合成。脊柱未陈述描述间的这种迁移。 / For arbitrary worlds, menus and comparisons: one review description covers another's clear certificates on all menus iff each defeat is reflected pairwise, and coverage composes. The spine bridges Robust to CommonResponse but states no transport between descriptions. |
| [WholePracticalRestriction](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/WholePracticalRestriction.lean) | `Becoming.Contextual.glues_iff_agree` | — | 任意局部比较族：相容当且仅当有共同整体关系，最小扩张，恰在覆盖对上唯一，严格循环阻碍。Context 全覆盖下粘合取值；部分覆盖的关系下传未陈述。 / For arbitrary families of local comparisons realized in wholes: compatibility iff a common whole relation, least extension, uniqueness exactly on covered pairs, strict-cycle obstruction. Becoming.Context glues values under full coverage; descent of relations under partial coverage is unstated. |
| [PurposeCorrection](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/noninstrumental-concern/PurposeCorrection.lean) | `Becoming.Viability.kernel_largest`, `Becoming.Viability.kernel_iff_certificate`, `Becoming.Lasting.reaches_iff_funnel` | — | 对每个过程、条件 K、终点 G：只留走向仍可在 K 内完成到 G 之状态的步，是保持完成的最大子过程。脊柱只有全称对偶（生存核）。 / For every process, condition K and terminal G: the productive restriction (steps into states with some K-guarded completion to G) is the largest completion-keeping subprocess; restriction creates no completion. The spine states the universal dual (kernel), not this existential one. |
| [SituatedPractice](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/noninstrumental-concern/SituatedPractice.lean) | `Becoming.commonResponse_judge_iff`, `Becoming.Readout.judges_iff_lumpedOn` | — | 对每个过程、整段历史的适合与读出：可靠回应存在恰当每个读法类有共同适合的延续，细化保持之。反向用经典选择。 / For every process, whole-history fit and readout: a sound reading-indexed response exists exactly when each reading class has a common fitting continuation; refinement keeps it. Its converse uses classical choice; a core form would list readings. |

### 控制、学习与估值 / Control, learning and valuation (9)

| 模块 / Module | 脊柱 / Spine | 关系 / Relation | 理由 / Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| [ClosedLoopValuation](../../../../experiments/valuation-development/lean/ClosedLoopValuation.lean) | `Becoming.Law.Merges.not_both_return`, `Becoming.Law.merge_draws` | — | 对每个组织与后果律，保留收缩时：选择一致的历史使差别收缩，放大必经选择不同之步。脊柱不陈述收缩何时迫使合并。 / For every organization and consequence law with a contracting retention: histories along which selections agree contract the difference, so amplification needs a step of disagreement. The spine states what merging does, not when contraction forces it. |
| [ExplorationCoverage](../../../../experiments/valuation-development/lean/ExplorationCoverage.lean) | `Becoming.Run.fixes_of_takes`, `Becoming.Run.not_fixes_of_untaken` | — | 对保留恰对所到选项的每个组织：到达者估计等于后果，未到达者保持初值，覆盖使估计相同。脊柱只陈述记录一侧。 / For every organization whose retention is exact for the option reached and silent on others: reached estimates equal consequences, unreached keep their start, and coverage, not its source, equalizes. The spine states the record side (Experiment), not this estimate law. |
| [ValuationBridges](../../../../experiments/valuation-development/lean/ValuationBridges.lean) | `Becoming.Weights.sum_courses`, `Becoming.Law.Absorbing.orbit` | — | 对每个带吸收集的有限加权核：每步至多 r/q 留在外，n 步后至多 (r/q)^n；账目对接续与分裂封闭。`Becoming.Weighted` 无此界。 / For every finite weighted kernel with an absorbing set: if at most r of q units stay outside per step, at most r^n of q^n stay after n steps; ledgers close under succession and division. `Becoming.Weighted` lacks this bound. |
| [ValuationDevelopment](../../../../experiments/valuation-development/lean/ValuationDevelopment.lean) | `Becoming.Law.Merges.not_both_return`, `Becoming.Web.selects_alike_iff` | — | 对每个组织与后果律：选择只经保留的差别改变；共同遭遇下逐次收缩使保留条件在算出的步数内合并。收缩律在脊柱上没有对应。 / Over every organization and consequence law: selection changes only through retained difference, and per-encounter contraction under shared exposure merges retained conditions within a computed number of steps; countermodels follow. The contraction law has no spine counterpart. |
| [ControlledRefinement](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/learning/ControlledRefinement.lean) | `Becoming.Bisimulation.comp`, `Becoming.Coupling` | — | 对每个过程：精确抽象（`LocalExact`）可组合，观察在纤维上一致时闭环后仍成立（`closed_loop_local_exact`），否则不成立。Bisimulation.comp 涵盖组合，闭环律未陈述。 / For every process, exact abstraction (`LocalExact`) composes and survives closing the loop with a controller when observations agree on fibres (`closed_loop_local_exact`); a witness shows it fails otherwise. Bisimulation.comp covers composition; the closed-loop law is not stated. |
| [ControllerRevision](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/learning/ControllerRevision.lean) | `Becoming.Web.selects_alike_iff`, `Becoming.Coupling` | — | 对每个带记忆的确定控制器闭环：记忆与指令由观察词确定（`same_feedback_same_commands`），相反未来需要不同反馈。Selection 把经登记的读取作为给定。 / For every closed loop of a deterministic controller with memory, memory and commands are fixed by the observation word (`same_feedback_same_commands`), so opposite futures need different feedback. Selection takes reading-through-registration as supplied; this derives it for controllers. |
| [ProtocolCompletion](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/learning/ProtocolCompletion.lean) | `Becoming.Run.not_of_depletion`, `Becoming.Stock.trace_falls`, `Becoming.Lasting` | — | 对每个具存续且降秩约定的协议：指令有至多秩加一个事件的完成块。无无限内部运行是 Run.not_of_depletion 的实例；一般过程中完成的存在未陈述。 / For every protocol with a live, rank-lowering contract, each issued command has a completed block of at most rank plus one events. No infinite internal run is an instance of Run.not_of_depletion; completion existence in a general process is not stated. |
| [ProtocolComposition](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/learning/ProtocolComposition.lean) | `Becoming.Frame.blocks_trace_iff`, `Becoming.Granularity` | — | 对每个过程与协议：宏步是提交指令下首次回到边界的路径，块轨迹恰是实现（`block_trace_iff_realization`）。Granularity 的块展开为固定词；可变首返块未陈述。 / For every process and protocol, macro steps are first-return paths to a boundary under a committed command, and block traces are exactly realizations (`block_trace_iff_realization`). Granularity's blocks expand each move to one fixed word; variable first-return blocks are not stated. |
| [RelationsAcrossScales](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/systems-complexity/analysis/RelationsAcrossScales.lean) | `Becoming.Tower`, `Becoming.Sampling` | — | 对历史的每个度量描述：每步缺差与增长界传成有限时域界（`one_step`、`finite_horizon_bound`、`composed_defect`）。Tower 把实值读法留给外围对应，此即其一。 / For every metric description of a history, per-step defect and growth bounds propagate to a finite-horizon bound (`one_step`, `finite_horizon_bound`, `composed_defect`). Tower defers real-valued readings to periphery correspondence; this would be one. |

### 所及、持续与有限否证 / Reach, persistence and finite refutation (3)

| 模块 / Module | 脊柱 / Spine | 关系 / Relation | 理由 / Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| [EnactedOrientation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/EnactedOrientation.lean) | `Becoming.Attraction`, `Becoming.Branching` | — | 对每个有限配置的过程：严格失去可达者的步至多与配置数一样多（`strict_loss_bounded`），无界复现的标记步中有返回者。Attraction 只陈述确定律的形式。 / For every process with finitely many configurations, a course of strictly reach-losing steps has at most that many steps (`strict_loss_bounded`), and an unboundedly recurring marked step includes one that returns. Attraction states the determined-law analogue (`enters_returning`). |
| [RegeneratingPersistence](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/RegeneratingPersistence.lean) | `Becoming.Branching`, `Becoming.Attraction` | — | 对每个有限状态过程：活动沿任意长接续保持最终可实现，恰当某可达配置经非空路径返回且实现它。最近者为 Branching 的 Returns.run。 / For every process with finitely many states, an activity stays eventually realizable along arbitrarily long continuations iff a reachable configuration returns by a nonempty path and realizes it. Branching's Returns.run and Attraction's enters_returning are the nearest spine laws. |
| [ContinuationScope](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/acquisition/feedback/remaining-dependency/formal/ContinuationScope.lean) | `Becoming.Bridge` | — | 对每个情境：只计入有两条路径相异的延续，不改变哪些路径无谓地关闭（`unreached_never_matters`）。Bridge 陈述未被支配的前沿（`frontier_exists`），无范围律。 / For every situation, counting only continuations some two courses differ on leaves needless foreclosure unchanged (`unreached_never_matters`); omitting a reached one distorts it. Bridge states the undominated frontier (`frontier_exists`, an analogue of `nonforeclosing_exists`) but no scope law. |

### 一般数学：完备、容许与真 / General mathematics: completion, admission and truth (18)：暂留脊柱之外（Ren，10 月 16 日） / outside the spine for now (Ren, 16 October)

| 模块 / Module | 脊柱 / Spine | 关系 / Relation | 理由 / Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| [Admission](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/admission/formal/Admission.lean) | `Becoming.Context` | — | 谓词类为全，恰当它含有限谓词且对非空有向并封闭（`full_iff_finite_directed`）；FiniteAdmission 与 FamilyAdmission 的一般形式。脊柱上无对应，Context 相距较远。 / A predicate class is full exactly when it includes the finite predicates and is closed under nonempty directed unions (`full_iff_finite_directed`); the general form of FiniteAdmission and FamilyAdmission. No spine counterpart; Context is distant. |
| [CompletionRole](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/completion/formal/CompletionRole.lean) | `Becoming.Histories` | — | 对任意偏序：理想完备的紧元恰是主理想，单调映射唯一延拓为连续映射，基决定其域至同构。与 Histories 相距远，后者无完备。 / For any poset, ideal completion's compact elements are exactly the principal ideals, monotone maps extend uniquely to continuous ones, and a basis determines its domain up to isomorphism. Distant from Histories, which states splice-closed history families, not completion. |
| [DenseOrder](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/order/formal/DenseOrder.lean) | `Becoming.Histories` | — | 无端点稠密线序中：序模式相同的赋值满足相同的相等与序公式，见证取自前、后与之间（`truth_of_pattern`）。EqualityGenerativity 的序情形；Histories 为远类比。 / In dense linear orders without endpoints, assignments with the same order pattern satisfy the same equality–order formulas, with witnesses from before, after and between (`truth_of_pattern`, `finite_witness_reduction`). The order case of EqualityGenerativity; Histories is a distant analogue. |
| [DependentCompletion](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/families/formal/DependentCompletion.lean) | `Becoming.Context` | — | 任意依赖族的截面对应于其依赖和的有限子集的全函数式理想，空纤维时无。FunctionGraphs 的依赖形式；Context 无此表示。 / Sections of any dependent family correspond to total functional ideals of finite subsets of its dependent sum; an empty fiber admits none. The dependent form of FunctionGraphs; Context lacks this representation. |
| [EqualityGenerativity](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/formal/EqualityGenerativity.lean) | `Becoming.Histories` | — | 纯相等语言中、载体有新元时：相等模式相同的赋值满足相同公式，存在见证化为有限多候选（`truth_of_pattern`）。Histories 的 `alike_iff_sameTests` 是最近而远的类比。 / In the pure equality language over carriers with fresh elements, assignments with the same equality pattern satisfy the same formulas, and existential witnesses reduce to finitely many candidates (`truth_of_pattern`, `finite_witness_reduction`). Histories' `alike_iff_sameTests` is the nearest, distant analogue. |
| [FamilyAdmission](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/families/formal/FamilyAdmission.lean) | `Becoming.Context` | — | 有限依赖和与截面上全准入成立；全等价于准入每个有限纤维族的支撑（`full_iff_finite_support`）。脊柱无对应，Context 远。 / Full admission holds on finite dependent sums and sections, and fullness equals admitting the support of every subsingleton or finite fiber family (`full_iff_finite_support`). No spine counterpart; Context is distant. |
| [FiniteAdmission](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/coverage/formal/FiniteAdmission.lean) | `Becoming.Context` | — | 在有限载体上：谓词类为全恰当含有限谓词，对空、单点与二元并封闭即足（`full_iff_finite`）。脊柱无谓词准入，Context 远。 / On a finite carrier, a predicate class is full exactly when it includes the finite predicates; closure under empty, singletons and binary union suffices (`full_iff_finite`). No spine counterpart; Context is distant. |
| [FunctionGraphs](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/admission/formal/FunctionGraphs.lean) | `Becoming.Context` | — | 全函数一一对应于有限关系图的全函数式理想（`decode_encode`）。Context 的 `glues_iff_agree` 粘合一致的局部读法，无有限图表示。 / Full functions correspond to total functional ideals of finite relation graphs (`decode_encode`, `total_iff_finite_coverage`). Context's `glues_iff_agree` glues agreeing local readings without a finite-graph representation. |
| [HistoryIdeals](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/completion/formal/HistoryIdeals.lean) | `Becoming.Histories` | — | 把有限字前缀序的理想分为主理想（有限历史）与轨迹理想（无限历史），序恰是前缀（`ideal_classification`）。Histories 的 `family_iff` 无此分类。 / Classifies ideals of the finite-word prefix order as principal (finite histories) or trace ideals (infinite), with order exactly prefix (`ideal_classification`, `history_order_iff`). Histories states law-generated families (`family_iff`) but no finite–infinite classification. |
| [IdealWarrant](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/completion/formal/IdealWarrant.lean) | `Becoming.Refutable.adheres_iff`, `Becoming.refutable_passes` | — | 对任意偏序：理想的性质恰在 Scott 闭时可有限否证，此时即由许可的基元素准入。其运行层情形已在脊柱上（`Becoming.Refutable.adheres_iff`、`Becoming.refutable_passes`，经 PrefixSafety）；它定义 CompletionRole 与 HistoryIdeals 所依的偏序与理想，与这条线留在一起（Ren，2026-10-16）。 / Over any poset, a property of ideals is finitely refutable exactly when Scott-closed, and then it is admission by a permitted set of basis elements (`scottClosed_iff_admits_permitted`). Its run-level case is on the spine (`Becoming.Refutable.adheres_iff`, `Becoming.refutable_passes`, through PrefixSafety). It defines the posets and ideals CompletionRole and HistoryIdeals build on, and stays with that line (Ren, 16 October 2026). |
| [IndexedAssembly](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/assembly/formal/IndexedAssembly.lean) | `Becoming.Context` | — | 在自然数上：谓词类为全恰当含有限谓词且对索引有限链之并封闭（`full_iff_finite_chain`）。脊柱无谓词准入，Context 远。 / On Nat, a predicate class is full exactly when it includes the finite predicates and is closed under unions of Nat-indexed finite chains (`full_iff_finite_chain`). No spine module states predicate admission; Context is distant. |
| [InductiveDomain](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/induction/formal/InductiveDomain.lean) | `Becoming.Lasting` | — | 对任一零与后继载体：完全归纳恰在每个元素被生成时成立（`fullInduction_iff_noJunk`）；加构造子分离即为自然数。Lasting 的 `avoids_iff_closed` 只用沿轨道的闭集。 / For any zero–successor carrier, full induction holds exactly when every element is generated (`fullInduction_iff_noJunk`); with constructor separation the carrier is Nat with unique recursive maps. Lasting's `avoids_iff_closed` uses closed sets along one orbit, not generation. |
| [Initiality](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/induction/formal/Initiality.lean) | `Becoming.Lasting` | — | 对零与后继载体：泛映射性质恰在其被生成且构造子分离时成立（`initial_iff_generated_separated`）。补全 InductiveDomain；最近为 Lasting 的闭轨道律，相距远。 / For a zero–successor carrier, the universal mapping property holds exactly when it is generated and its constructors separated (`initial_iff_generated_separated`). Completes InductiveDomain; the nearest spine laws are Lasting's closed-orbit laws, distantly. |
| [PotentialTruth](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/potential/formal/PotentialTruth.lean) | `Becoming.Horizon` | — | 对具未来覆盖的任一框架、以未来域量化：潜在真等于全局真，沿未来不变（`potential_iff_global`）；无有限前缀世界是全的。Horizon 只陈述特定前缀情形。 / With future-domain quantifiers over any frame with future coverage, potential truth equals truth in the global interpretation and is invariant along the future (`potential_iff_global`); no finite prefix world is full. Horizon states only specific prefix cases. |
| [SecondOrder](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/higher-order/formal/SecondOrder.lean) | `Becoming.Histories` | — | 任意有限元数的关系二阶公式：在相关的域下，满足在谓词语义与理想语义间传递，全域相关（`full_satisfaction_transport`）。Histories 的 `alike_iff_sameTests` 是远类比。 / For relational second-order formulas of any finite arity, satisfaction transports between predicate and ideal semantics over related domains, and full domains are related (`full_satisfaction_transport`). Histories' `alike_iff_sameTests` is the nearest, distant analogue. |
| [SignatureTruth](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/coverage/formal/SignatureTruth.lean) | `Becoming.Histories` | — | 对任意类型签名与理论：真、模型与语义蕴涵在直接解释与图完备解释间传递（`entailment_transport`）。Histories 的 `alike_iff_sameTests` 是远类比。 / For arbitrary typed signatures and theories, truth, models and semantic entailment transport between direct and graph-completed interpretations (`entailment_transport`). Histories' `alike_iff_sameTests` is the nearest, distant analogue. |
| [TypedTruth](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/higher-order/formal/TypedTruth.lean) | `Becoming.Histories` | — | 有限简单类型中：项与公式沿任一基双射在直接解释与谓词完备解释间传递（`formula_transport`）。Histories 的 `alike_iff_sameTests` 是远类比；SignatureTruth 推广之。 / For finite simple types, terms and formulas transport along any base bijection between direct and predicate-completed interpretations (`formula_transport`, `sentence_transport`). Histories' `alike_iff_sameTests` is the nearest, distant analogue; SignatureTruth extends it to arbitrary signatures. |
| [WitnessHull](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/constitution/formal/WitnessHull.lean) | `Becoming.Histories` | — | 任意语言的 Tarski–Vaught：嵌入恰在见证充分时为初等，有穷见证闭包是初等子结构（`elementary_iff_witness`）。Histories 的 `alike_iff_sameTests` 是最近而远的类比。 / Tarski–Vaught for any language: an embedding is elementary exactly when witness-adequate, and finitary witness hulls are elementary substructures (`elementary_iff_witness`, `hull_elementary`). Histories' `alike_iff_sameTests` is the nearest, distant analogue of preserving all tests. |

### 推导演算 / Derivation calculus (2)：暂留脊柱之外（Ren，10 月 16 日） / outside the spine for now (Ren, 16 October)

| 模块 / Module | 脊柱 / Spine | 关系 / Relation | 理由 / Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| [CoreTreeRearticulation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/inquiry-root/CoreTreeRearticulation.lean) | `Becoming.Bridge.derivation_sound_on_scope`, `Becoming.Bridge.closure_over_scope_iff_basis` | — | 对继承的 Derives 演算的每个基础：两基础在每个语境推出相同，恰当彼此推出对方成员。桥接已在 Scope 上读 Derives，但未陈述此替换律。 / States, for every basis of the inherited Derives calculus, that two bases derive alike in every context exactly when each derives the other's members. The bridge reads Derives over Scope but not this basis-replacement law. |
| [WholeTheoryDischarge](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/inquiry-root/WholeTheoryDischarge.lean) | `Becoming.Bridge.derivation_sound_on_scope`, `Becoming.Bridge.closure_over_scope_iff_basis` | — | 对每个 Derives 基础：沿所给良基序推出的根可消解为空基础，并构造性重证健全性。最近处是桥接的作用域推导部分，可补此消解律。 / States, for every Derives basis, that roots derived along a supplied well-founded order discharge to the empty basis, and reproves soundness constructively. Nearest is the bridge's derivation-over-scope section; the discharge law would be added. |

## 与 P3 分类的对照 / Against the P3 classification

[P3 的分类](../history/root-foundation/BECOMING-INSTANCE-CLASSIFICATION.md)（10 月 9 日）归类了 46 个含类型多态定理的伦理模块；其中 24 个在脊柱运行之外，见于本表，逐行标出其 P3 类别。P3 的“已在核心上”指陈述直接写在基础的 `Process`／`Trace` 上，不指某条脊柱律陈述了它；本表给这些模块补上所及的脊柱声明。

[The P3 classification](../history/root-foundation/BECOMING-INSTANCE-CLASSIFICATION.md) (9 October) sorted the 46 ethics modules with type-polymorphic theorems. 24 of them lie outside the spine run and appear here, each row naming its P3 category. P3’s “already on the core” means stated directly on the foundation’s `Process` and `Trace`, not that a spine law states it; this table adds the spine declarations those modules reach.

### lean/conditional (12)

| 模块 / Module | 定理 / Thms | 所及 / Reaches | 类别 / Category | 脊柱 / Spine | 依据 / Basis | 理由 / Reason |
|---|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| [NativePortContinuation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/native-port/NativePortContinuation.lean) | 27 | foundation only | application | `GenerativeCore` | header read | 把原生端口读作 ResourceContinuation 的所持、所化、所来与 Depletion 的实例；每次原生调用无论完成或中断都保持该计量。是外围规律的实例，不是脊柱的。 / The native port read as an instance of ResourceContinuation's held, enacted and arrived quantities and of Depletion; every native call, completed or interrupted, keeps the account. Instance of periphery laws, not of the spine. |
| [RenewalAccounting](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalAccounting.lean) | 12 | foundation only | join candidate | `Becoming.Generation`, `Becoming.Interdependence` | statements read | 对每个有限类约束–流之律：各类寿命内打开少于形成所需时，更新势每拍下降，没有历史永远更新。是 RenewalCharacterization 期限的情形；脊柱无此计量。 / For every finite-kind constraint–flow law whose kinds open less over their spans than forming them demands, a renewal potential falls each tick, so no history renews forever. A case of the deadline in RenewalCharacterization; the spine states no such accounting. |
| [RenewalBasics](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalBasics.lean) | 35 | none | application | — | statements read | 支持更新诸模块的不用公理的列表与自然数事实库；没有理论内容。核心有自己的不用公理的算术（例如 Dependence.add_sub_of_le 陈述 `add_sub_of_le_eq`）。 / An axiom-free library of list and natural-number facts supporting the renewal modules; no theory content. The core keeps its own axiom-free arithmetic (for example Dependence.add_sub_of_le states `add_sub_of_le_eq`). |
| [RenewalCharacterization](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalCharacterization.lean) | 55 | foundation only | join candidate | `Becoming.Generation`, `Becoming.Interdependence` | statements read | 更新–闭合定理：对有限类约束–流之律，有历史永远存续，恰当有非空种群在持久支持下闭合且所开不少于形成所需，否则有期限。 / Renewal–closure theorem: for every finite-kind constraint–flow law, some history stays live forever iff a nonempty population is closed under lasting support and opens at least its formation demand; otherwise an explicit deadline. The spine states only necessity-side laws. |
| [RenewalClosure](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalClosure.lean) | 21 | foundation only | join candidate | `Becoming.Descent`, `Becoming.Wholes` | statements read | 对每个有限类、支持范围有界的系统：足够晚的发生源自已实现的支持循环（闭合），无环支持有期限。脊柱的 Embodied.descends 只给一步世系。 / For every occurrence-support system with finitely many kinds and bounded support reach, any late occurrence descends from a realized support cycle (a closure), so acyclic support has a deadline. The spine's Embodied.descends gives one-step descent only. |
| [RenewalConfigurations](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalConfigurations.lean) | 29 | foundation only | join candidate | `Becoming.Viability.kernel_step_iff` | statements read | 对每个通道律：配置可更新恰当它存续且一步到可更新者；可更新性从不生成。Viability 只有全称的生存核（kernel_step_iff），无此存在形式。 / For every channel law, a configuration is renewable iff live with a step to a renewable one, and renewability is never generated along a history. Viability states only the universal kernel form (`kernel_step_iff`), not this existential one. |
| [RenewalExamples](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/RenewalExamples.lean) | 21 | foundation only | application | `GenerativeCore` | header read | 为 ResourceContinuation 的维持律给出有限实现与边界情形（更新循环、终止分支、到达上限）；见证相容性并排除更强的结论。 / Finite realizations and boundary cases for ResourceContinuation's sustaining law (renewal cycle, terminal branch, arrival cap); they witness compatibility and rule out stronger conclusions. |
| [RenewalLink](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalLink.lean) | 16 | foundation only | join candidate | `Becoming.Descent` | statements read | 在同一执行上连接 RenewalAccounting 与 RenewalClosure：每个记号在确定的较早一拍形成，故足够晚的存续配置源自已实现的闭合。是更新–闭合定理的组成部分。 / Joins RenewalAccounting and RenewalClosure on one execution: every token was formed on a definite earlier tick, so a live configuration late enough descends from a realized closure. A component of the renewal–closure theorem. |
| [RenewalPremises](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalPremises.lean) | 19 | foundation only | application | — | statements read | 说明其余前提必要的反例：类以自然数为索引时，一条链没有自更新种群也永远更新；零需求使初始期限不成立。 / Counterexamples showing the remaining premises are needed: with kinds indexed by the naturals a chain renews forever without a self-renewing population, and zero demand defeats an initial deadline. |
| [RenewalSufficiency](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/renewal/RenewalSufficiency.lean) | 32 | foundation only | join candidate | `Becoming.Generation` | statements read | 充分性：对每个通道律，持久支持下闭合且打开覆盖形成的非空种群有每拍返回的稳态配置，故永远更新。脊柱的 Generation 在必要性一侧。 / Sufficiency direction: for every channel law, a nonempty population closed under lasting support whose openings cover its formations has a steady configuration that returns each tick, so it renews forever. The spine's Generation laws are necessity-side. |
| [ResourceContinuation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/ResourceContinuation.lean) | 24 | foundation only | join candidate | `Becoming.Stock` | statements read | 对每个过程：每次转移后所持加所化不超过之前所持加所来（容许损失），界定轨迹、到达窗口与频率。Stock 只有等式平衡与单纯下降。 / For every process, a per-transition law held-after plus enacted at most held-before plus arrived (losses allowed) bounds every trace, arrival windows and frequencies. Stock states equality balance (`trace_balance`), pure falling (`trace_falls`) and `replenishes_of_draws`, not this inequality form. |
| [SupportNecessity](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/SupportNecessity.lean) | 18 | foundation only | join candidate | `Becoming.Descent` | statements read | 对出生需先前仍运作之亲代的每个情节系统：有限寿命与支持类型上有界严格秩给出期限。RenewalClosure 去掉了秩；脊柱的 Embodied.descends 没有期限。 / For every episode system whose births need an earlier functioning parent, finite lifetimes and a bounded strict rank on support kinds give a deadline. RenewalClosure removes the rank; the spine's Embodied.descends states descent but no deadline. |

| [EmbeddedEvaluation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/EmbeddedEvaluation.lean) | 5 | inherited module | join candidate | `Becoming.Unfolding` | statements read | 初等的一般规律：每步按一关系更新的记录恰等于该关系沿整段历史成立（`record_tracks_whole_history`），且不改变可行性。脊柱未陈述此律。 / An elementary general law: a record updated by a relation across each step equals that relation along the whole history (`record_tracks_whole_history`) and changes no enabledness or feasibility. The spine's withMove and Entropy records state no such law. |
| [EnactedOrientation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/EnactedOrientation.lean) | 27 | inherited module | join candidate | `Becoming.Attraction`, `Becoming.Branching` | statements read | 对每个有限配置的过程：严格失去可达者的步至多与配置数一样多（`strict_loss_bounded`），无界复现的标记步中有返回者。Attraction 只陈述确定律的形式。 / For every process with finitely many configurations, a course of strictly reach-losing steps has at most that many steps (`strict_loss_bounded`), and an unboundedly recurring marked step includes one that returns. Attraction states the determined-law analogue (`enters_returning`). |
| [NaturalismIdentity](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/NaturalismIdentity.lean) | 20 | inherited module | application | `DynamicConsequences` | statements read | 在 dip、novel、incomparable 诸律上：自然主义判据容许接续序及其逆，固定范围容许损失，全序有所削弱。其一般的 `nonUndermining_whole_iff` 是定义性的。脊柱没有方向序。 / On the dip, novel and incomparable laws: naturalist criteria admit the continuation order and its converse, fixed scopes permit loss, total orders undermine. Its general `nonUndermining_whole_iff` is definitional. No spine orientation order exists. |
| [NaturalismReview](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/NaturalismReview.lean) | 10 | inherited module | application | `DynamicConsequences` | statements read | 审查后的更正：结构判据对每个关系成立（`credentials_hold_for_every_relation`，供 PremiseNecessity 使用）；switching、committing、dip 见证显示全序与接续序把节制、选择与恢复算作损害。 / Review corrections: the structural credentials hold for every relation (`credentials_hold_for_every_relation`, used by PremiseNecessity), and switching, committing and dip witnesses show total orders and the continuation order condemning restraint, choice and recovery. |
| [PremiseNecessity](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/PremiseNecessity.lean) | 9 | inherited module | join candidate | `Becoming.Metaethics` | statements read | 对每个过程，接续序与其逆都具有前序、随附与组合判据，故需要方向输入。Metaethics 为种子与中立给出必要性见证，没有方向这一项。 / For every process, the continuation order and its converse both carry the preorder, supervenience and composition credentials, so an orientation input is needed. Metaethics states necessity witnesses for its own premises (seed, neutralities), not for orientation. |
| [ProtectedScope](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/ProtectedScope.lean) | 35 | inherited module | application | `DynamicConsequences`, `Becoming.Reach.trans` | statements read | 由最终可实现性定义受保护与损害的活动，并在节制、承诺、dip 与竞争诸律上检验。其一般引理（可实现性沿历史不增）是 Reach.trans 的实例。 / Defines protected and damaging activity from eventual realizability and tests it on restraint, commitment, dip and rival laws. Its general lemma, realizability never increasing along a history, is an instance of Reach.trans. |
| [ProtectedScopeLimits](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/ProtectedScopeLimits.lean) | 6 | inherited module | application | `DynamicConsequences` | header read | ProtectedScope 审查中的反例：挽救承诺的范围不挽救节制，逃离可以不被登记地去掉伤害，dip 降到零不算损害。 / Counterexamples from review of ProtectedScope: the scope that rescues commitment does not rescue restraint, an escape can remove harm unregistered, a dip fall to zero is not damage. |
| [RegeneratingPersistence](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/RegeneratingPersistence.lean) | 10 | inherited module | join candidate | `Becoming.Branching`, `Becoming.Attraction` | statements read | 对每个有限状态过程：活动沿任意长接续保持最终可实现，恰当某可达配置经非空路径返回且实现它。最近者为 Branching 的 Returns.run。 / For every process with finitely many states, an activity stays eventually realizable along arbitrarily long continuations iff a reachable configuration returns by a nonempty path and realizes it. Branching's Returns.run and Attraction's enters_returning are the nearest spine laws. |
| [WholeQualityNaturalism](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/WholeQualityNaturalism.lean) | 25 | inherited module | earlier version | `Becoming.Readout.reading_single_iff`, `Becoming.Change.persists` (spine-generalizes) | statements read | `supervenes_factors`（随附于基的性质经基分解）即 Readout.reading_single_iff；`loss_localizes`（失去的可行性在某一步失去）是 Change.persists 的逆否。dip 见证仍是应用。 / `supervenes_factors` (a property supervening on a base factors through it) is Readout.reading_single_iff; `loss_localizes` (a lost feasibility is lost at some step) is the contrapositive of Change.persists. The dip witnesses remain applications. |
